# Free Flight bank: prompts used outside the Daily Sortie (Classic, Endurance).
# Same format as prompts.txt. Run `npm run prompts` to compile to src/data/free-prompts.json.
# Header:  @ id | category | prompt text
# Tiers:   R Rookie 10 · S Showboat 15 · W Wingman 30 · A Ace 60 · X Stealth 85 · B Sonic Boom 100
# Answers are separated by ";", aliases by "/" (first form is the display text).
